        software]
        software/config=6/

       [Number of times a process has migrated to a new CPU [This event is an
        alias of cpu-migrations]. Unit: software]
        software/config=4/

       [Number of minor page faults. Minor faults don't require I/O to handle.
        Unit: software]
        software/config=5/

       [Number of page faults [This event is an alias of faults]. Unit:
        software]
        software/config=2/
  task-clock

        software/config=1/

  unc_arb_coh_trk_occupancy.all
       [Each cycle count number of valid entries in Coherency Tracker queue

        uncore_arb/event=0x83,umask=1/

        aperture,etc. Unit: uncore_arb]
        uncore_arb/event=0x84,umask=1/
  unc_arb_trk_occupancy.all
       [Each cycle counts number of all Core outgoing valid entries. Such
        entry is defined as valid from its allocation till first of IDI0 or
        DRS0 messages is sent out. Accounts for Coherent and non-coherent
        traffic. Unit: uncore_arb]
        uncore_arb/event=0x80,umask=1/
  unc_arb_trk_occupancy.cycles_with_any_request
       [Cycles with at least one request outstanding is waiting for data

       [Cycle count for an Extended Page table walk. Unit: cpu]
        cpu/event=0x4f,period=2000003,umask=0x10/
  itlb.itlb_flush
       [Flushing of the Instruction TLB (ITLB) pages,includes 4k/2M/4M pages.
        Unit: cpu]
        cpu/event=0xae,period=100003,umask=1/
  itlb_misses.miss_causes_a_walk
       [Misses at all ITLB levels that cause page walks. Unit: cpu]
        cpu/event=0x85,period=100003,umask=1/
  itlb_misses.stlb_hit
       [Operations that miss the first ITLB level but hit the second and do
        not cause any page walks. Unit: cpu]
        cpu/event=0x85,period=100003,umask=0x60/
  itlb_misses.stlb_hit_2m
       [Code misses that miss the DTLB and hit the STLB (2M). Unit: cpu]
        cpu/event=0x85,period=100003,umask=0x40/
  itlb_misses.stlb_hit_4k
       [Core misses that miss the DTLB and hit the STLB (4K). Unit: cpu]
        cpu/event=0x85,period=100003,umask=0x20/
  itlb_misses.walk_completed
       [Misses in all ITLB levels that cause completed page walks. Unit: cpu]
        cpu/event=0x85,period=100003,umask=0xe/
  itlb_misses.walk_completed_1g
       [Store miss in all TLB levels causes a page walk that completes. (1G).
        Unit: cpu]
        cpu/event=0x85,period=100003,umask=8/
  itlb_misses.walk_completed_2m_4m
       [Code miss in all TLB levels causes a page walk that completes.
        (2M/4M). Unit: cpu]
        cpu/event=0x85,period=100003,umask=4/
  itlb_misses.walk_completed_4k
       [Code miss in all TLB levels causes a page walk that completes. (4K).
        Unit: cpu]
        cpu/event=0x85,period=100003,umask=2/
  itlb_misses.walk_duration
       [Cycles when PMH is busy with page walks. Unit: cpu]
        cpu/event=0x85,period=100003,umask=0x10/
  page_walker_loads.dtlb_l1
       [Number of DTLB page walker hits in the L1+FB. Unit: cpu]
        cpu/event=0xbc,period=2000003,umask=0x11/
  page_walker_loads.dtlb_l2
       [Number of DTLB page walker hits in the L2. Unit: cpu]
        cpu/event=0xbc,period=2000003,umask=0x12/
  page_walker_loads.dtlb_l3
       [Number of DTLB page walker hits in the L3 + XSNP Spec update: HSD25.
        Unit: cpu]
        cpu/event=0xbc,period=2000003,umask=0x14/
  page_walker_loads.dtlb_memory
       [Number of DTLB page walker hits in Memory Spec update: HSD25. Unit:
        cpu]
        cpu/event=0xbc,period=2000003,umask=0x18/
  page_walker_loads.ept_dtlb_l1
       [Counts the number of Extended Page Table walks from the DTLB that hit
        in the L1 and FB. Unit: cpu]
        cpu/event=0xbc,period=2000003,umask=0x41/
  page_walker_loads.ept_dtlb_l2
       [Counts the number of Extended Page Table walks from the DTLB that hit
        in the L2. Unit: cpu]
        cpu/event=0xbc,period=2000003,umask=0x42/
  page_walker_loads.ept_dtlb_l3
       [Counts the number of Extended Page Table walks from the DTLB that hit
        in the L3. Unit: cpu]
        cpu/event=0xbc,period=2000003,umask=0x44/

       [Counts the number of Extended Page Table walks from the DTLB that hit
        in memory. Unit: cpu]
        cpu/event=0xbc,period=2000003,umask=0x48/
  page_walker_loads.ept_itlb_l1
       [Counts the number of Extended Page Table walks from the ITLB that hit
        in the L1 and FB. Unit: cpu]
        cpu/event=0xbc,period=2000003,umask=0x81/
  page_walker_loads.ept_itlb_l2
       [Counts the number of Extended Page Table walks from the ITLB that hit
        in the L2. Unit: cpu]
        cpu/event=0xbc,period=2000003,umask=0x82/
  page_walker_loads.ept_itlb_l3
       [Counts the number of Extended Page Table walks from the ITLB that hit
        in the L2. Unit: cpu]
        cpu/event=0xbc,period=2000003,umask=0x84/
  page_walker_loads.ept_itlb_memory
       [Counts the number of Extended Page Table walks from the ITLB that hit
        in memory. Unit: cpu]
        cpu/event=0xbc,period=2000003,umask=0x88/
  page_walker_loads.itlb_l1
       [Number of ITLB page walker hits in the L1+FB. Unit: cpu]
        cpu/event=0xbc,period=2000003,umask=0x21/
  page_walker_loads.itlb_l2
       [Number of ITLB page walker hits in the L2. Unit: cpu]
        cpu/event=0xbc,period=2000003,umask=0x22/
  page_walker_loads.itlb_l3
       [Number of ITLB page walker hits in the L3 + XSNP Spec update: HSD25.
        Unit: cpu]
        cpu/event=0xbc,period=2000003,umask=0x24/
  page_walker_loads.itlb_memory
       [Number of ITLB page walker hits in Memory Spec update: HSD25. Unit:
        cpu]
        cpu/event=0xbc,period=2000003,umask=0x28/
  tlb_flush.dtlb_thread
       [DTLB flush attempts of the thread-specific entries. Unit: cpu]
        cpu/event=0xbd,period=100003,umask=1/
  tlb_flush.stlb_any
       [STLB flush attempts. Unit: cpu]
        cpu/event=0xbd,period=100003,umask=0x20/

Metric Groups:

Backend: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_core_bound
       [This metric represents fraction of slots where Core non-memory issues
        were of a bottleneck]
       [tma_backend_bound - tma_memory_bound]
       [tma_core_bound > 0.1 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2]
  tma_info_core_ilp
       [Instruction-Level-Parallelism (average number of uops executed when
        there is execution) per thread (logical-processor)]
       [(UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE / 2 / (cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=1@ / 2 if
        #SMT_on else cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=1@) if #SMT_on else
        UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE / (cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=1@ / 2 if #SMT_on
        else cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=1@))]
  tma_info_memory_l2mpki
       [L2 cache true misses per kilo instruction for retired demand loads]
       [1e3 * MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L2_MISS / INST_RETIRED.ANY]
  tma_memory_bound
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the Memory subsystem within
        the Backend was a bottleneck]
       [(min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,CYCLE_ACTIVITY.STALLS_LDM_PENDING) +
        RESOURCE_STALLS.SB) / (min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,
        CYCLE_ACTIVITY.CYCLES_NO_EXECUTE) + (cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=1@
        - (cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=3@ if tma_info_thread_ipc > 1.8 else
        cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=2@)) / 2 - (RS_EVENTS.EMPTY_CYCLES if
        tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 else 0) + RESOURCE_STALLS.SB if #SMT_on else
        min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,CYCLE_ACTIVITY.CYCLES_NO_EXECUTE) +
        cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=1@ - (cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=3@
        if tma_info_thread_ipc > 1.8 else cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=2@) -
        (RS_EVENTS.EMPTY_CYCLES if tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 else 0) +
        RESOURCE_STALLS.SB) * tma_backend_bound]
       [tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2]

Bad:
  tma_info_bad_spec_ipmisp_indirect
       [Instructions per retired Mispredicts for indirect CALL or JMP branches
        (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_instructions / (UOPS_RETIRED.RETIRE_SLOTS /
        UOPS_ISSUED.ANY * BR_MISP_EXEC.INDIRECT)]
       [tma_info_bad_spec_ipmisp_indirect < 1e3]
  tma_info_bad_spec_ipmispredict
       [Number of Instructions per non-speculative Branch Misprediction
        (JEClear) (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [INST_RETIRED.ANY / BR_MISP_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES]
       [tma_info_bad_spec_ipmispredict < 200]

BadSpec: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_branch_mispredicts
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU has wasted due to
        Branch Misprediction]
       [BR_MISP_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES / (BR_MISP_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES +
        MACHINE_CLEARS.COUNT) * tma_bad_speculation]
       [tma_branch_mispredicts > 0.1 & tma_bad_speculation > 0.15]
  tma_info_bad_spec_ipmispredict
       [Number of Instructions per non-speculative Branch Misprediction
        (JEClear) (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [INST_RETIRED.ANY / BR_MISP_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES]
       [tma_info_bad_spec_ipmispredict < 200]
  tma_machine_clears
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU has wasted due to
        Machine Clears]
       [tma_bad_speculation - tma_branch_mispredicts]
       [tma_machine_clears > 0.1 & tma_bad_speculation > 0.15]

BigFootprint: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_icache_misses
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        instruction cache misses]
       [ICACHE.IFDATA_STALL / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_icache_misses > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)]
  tma_itlb_misses
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        Instruction TLB (ITLB) misses]
       [(14 * ITLB_MISSES.STLB_HIT + ITLB_MISSES.WALK_DURATION) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_itlb_misses > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound
        > 0.15)]

BrMispredicts: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_branch_mispredicts
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU has wasted due to
        Branch Misprediction]
       [BR_MISP_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES / (BR_MISP_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES +
        MACHINE_CLEARS.COUNT) * tma_bad_speculation]
       [tma_branch_mispredicts > 0.1 & tma_bad_speculation > 0.15]
  tma_info_bad_spec_ipmisp_indirect
       [Instructions per retired Mispredicts for indirect CALL or JMP branches
        (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_instructions / (UOPS_RETIRED.RETIRE_SLOTS /
        UOPS_ISSUED.ANY * BR_MISP_EXEC.INDIRECT)]
       [tma_info_bad_spec_ipmisp_indirect < 1e3]
  tma_info_bad_spec_ipmispredict
       [Number of Instructions per non-speculative Branch Misprediction
        (JEClear) (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [INST_RETIRED.ANY / BR_MISP_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES]
       [tma_info_bad_spec_ipmispredict < 200]

Branches: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_info_frontend_tbpc
       [Taken Branches retired Per Cycle]
       [BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_TAKEN / tma_info_thread_clks]
  tma_info_inst_mix_bptkbranch
       [Branch instructions per taken branch]
       [BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES / BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_TAKEN]
  tma_info_inst_mix_ipbranch
       [Instructions per Branch (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [INST_RETIRED.ANY / BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_ipbranch < 8]
  tma_info_inst_mix_ipcall
       [Instructions per (near) call (lower number means higher occurrence
        rate)]
       [INST_RETIRED.ANY / BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_CALL]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_ipcall < 200]
  tma_info_inst_mix_iptb
       [Instructions per taken branch]
       [INST_RETIRED.ANY / BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_TAKEN]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_iptb < 9]
  tma_info_system_ipfarbranch
       [Instructions per Far Branch ( Far Branches apply upon transition from
        application to operating system,handling interrupts,exceptions) [lower
        number means higher occurrence rate]]
       [INST_RETIRED.ANY / BR_INST_RETIRED.FAR_BRANCH:u]
       [tma_info_system_ipfarbranch < 1e6]
  tma_info_thread_uptb
       [Uops per taken branch]
       [UOPS_RETIRED.RETIRE_SLOTS / BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_TAKEN]
       [tma_info_thread_uptb < 6]

BvBC: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_icache_misses
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        instruction cache misses]
       [ICACHE.IFDATA_STALL / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_icache_misses > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)]
  tma_itlb_misses
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        Instruction TLB (ITLB) misses]
       [(14 * ITLB_MISSES.STLB_HIT + ITLB_MISSES.WALK_DURATION) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_itlb_misses > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound
        > 0.15)]

BvCB: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_divider
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles where the Divider unit was
        active]
       [10 * ARITH.DIVIDER_UOPS / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_divider > 0.2 & (tma_core_bound > 0.1 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2)]
  tma_ports_utilized_3m
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles CPU executed total of 3 or
        more uops per cycle on all execution ports (Logical Processor cycles
        since ICL,Physical Core cycles otherwise)]
       [(cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=3@ / 2 if #SMT_on else
        cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=3@) / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_ports_utilized_3m > 0.4 & (tma_ports_utilization > 0.15 &
        (tma_core_bound > 0.1 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]

BvFB: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_frontend_bound
       [This category represents fraction of slots where the processor's
        Frontend undersupplies its Backend]
       [IDQ_UOPS_NOT_DELIVERED.CORE / tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_frontend_bound > 0.15]

BvIO: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_assists
       [This metric estimates fraction of slots the CPU retired uops delivered
        by the Microcode_Sequencer as a result of Assists]
       [66 * OTHER_ASSISTS.ANY_WB_ASSIST / tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_assists > 0.1 & (tma_microcode_sequencer > 0.05 &
        tma_heavy_operations > 0.1)]
  tma_frontend_bound
       [This category represents fraction of slots where the processor's
        Frontend undersupplies its Backend]
       [IDQ_UOPS_NOT_DELIVERED.CORE / tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_frontend_bound > 0.15]

BvMB: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_fb_full
       [This metric does a *rough estimation* of how often L1D Fill Buffer
        unavailability limited additional L1D miss memory access requests to
        proceed]
       [tma_info_memory_load_miss_real_latency *
        cpu@L1D_PEND_MISS.REQUEST_FB_FULL\,cmask\=1@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_fb_full > 0.3]
  tma_mem_bandwidth
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles where the core's performance
        was likely hurt due to approaching bandwidth limits of external memory
        - DRAM ([SPR-HBM] and/or HBM)]
       [min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,
        cpu@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.ALL_DATA_RD\,cmask\=6@) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_mem_bandwidth > 0.2 & (tma_dram_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_sq_full
       [This metric measures fraction of cycles where the Super Queue (SQ) was
        full taking into account all request-types and both hardware SMT
        threads (Logical Processors)]
       [(OFFCORE_REQUESTS_BUFFER.SQ_FULL / 2 if #SMT_on else
        OFFCORE_REQUESTS_BUFFER.SQ_FULL) / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_sq_full > 0.3 & (tma_l3_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]

BvML: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_l2_bound
       [This metric estimates how often the CPU was stalled due to L2 cache
        accesses by loads]
       [(CYCLE_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L1D_PENDING - CYCLE_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L2_PENDING)
        / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_l2_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.2)]
  tma_l3_hit_latency
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles with demand load accesses
        that hit the L3 cache under unloaded scenarios (possibly L3 latency
        limited)]
       [29 * (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT * (1 +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.HIT_LFB / (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L2_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT + MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HITM +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_MISS +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_MISS))) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_l3_hit_latency > 0.1 & (tma_l3_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_mem_latency
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles where the performance was
        likely hurt due to latency from external memory - DRAM ([SPR-HBM]
        and/or HBM)]
       [min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,
        OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DATA_RD) /
        tma_info_thread_clks - tma_mem_bandwidth]
       [tma_mem_latency > 0.1 & (tma_dram_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_store_latency
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles the CPU spent handling L1D
        store misses]
       [(L2_RQSTS.RFO_HIT * 9 * (1 - MEM_UOPS_RETIRED.LOCK_LOADS /
        MEM_UOPS_RETIRED.ALL_STORES) + (1 - MEM_UOPS_RETIRED.LOCK_LOADS /
        MEM_UOPS_RETIRED.ALL_STORES) * min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,
        OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DEMAND_RFO)) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_store_latency > 0.1 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]

BvMP: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_branch_mispredicts
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU has wasted due to
        Branch Misprediction]
       [BR_MISP_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES / (BR_MISP_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES +
        MACHINE_CLEARS.COUNT) * tma_bad_speculation]
       [tma_branch_mispredicts > 0.1 & tma_bad_speculation > 0.15]

BvMS: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_contested_accesses
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles while the memory subsystem
        was handling synchronizations due to contested accesses]
       [(60 * (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HITM * (1 +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.HIT_LFB / (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L2_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT + MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HITM +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_MISS +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_MISS))) + 43 *
        (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_MISS * (1 +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.HIT_LFB / (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L2_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT + MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HITM +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_MISS +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_MISS)))) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_contested_accesses > 0.05 & (tma_l3_bound > 0.05 &
        (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_data_sharing
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles while the memory subsystem
        was handling synchronizations due to data-sharing accesses]
       [43 * (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HIT * (1 +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.HIT_LFB / (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L2_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT + MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HITM +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_MISS +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_MISS))) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_data_sharing > 0.05 & (tma_l3_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_false_sharing
       [This metric roughly estimates how often CPU was handling
        synchronizations due to False Sharing]
       [60 * OFFCORE_RESPONSE.DEMAND_RFO.L3_HIT.HITM_OTHER_CORE /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_false_sharing > 0.05 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound
        > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_machine_clears
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU has wasted due to
        Machine Clears]
       [tma_bad_speculation - tma_branch_mispredicts]
       [tma_machine_clears > 0.1 & tma_bad_speculation > 0.15]

BvMT: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_dtlb_load
       [This metric roughly estimates the fraction of cycles where the Data
        TLB (DTLB) was missed by load accesses]
       [(8 * DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.STLB_HIT + DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_DURATION) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_dtlb_load > 0.1 & (tma_l1_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_dtlb_store
       [This metric roughly estimates the fraction of cycles spent handling
        first-level data TLB store misses]
       [(8 * DTLB_STORE_MISSES.STLB_HIT + DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_DURATION) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_dtlb_store > 0.05 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]

BvOB: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_backend_bound
       [This category represents fraction of slots where no uops are being
        delivered due to a lack of required resources for accepting new uops
        in the Backend]
       [1 - (tma_frontend_bound + tma_bad_speculation + tma_retiring)]
       [tma_backend_bound > 0.2]

BvUW: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_retiring
       [This category represents fraction of slots utilized by useful work
        i.e. issued uops that eventually get retired]
       [UOPS_RETIRED.RETIRE_SLOTS / tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_retiring > 0.7 | tma_heavy_operations > 0.1]

CacheHits: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_info_memory_l1mpki
       [L1 cache true misses per kilo instruction for retired demand loads]
       [1e3 * MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L1_MISS / INST_RETIRED.ANY]
  tma_info_memory_l2mpki
       [L2 cache true misses per kilo instruction for retired demand loads]
       [1e3 * MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L2_MISS / INST_RETIRED.ANY]
  tma_l1_bound
       [This metric estimates how often the CPU was stalled without loads
        missing the L1 Data (L1D) cache]
       [max((min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,CYCLE_ACTIVITY.STALLS_LDM_PENDING) -
        CYCLE_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L1D_PENDING) / tma_info_thread_clks,0)]
       [tma_l1_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.2)]
  tma_l2_bound
       [This metric estimates how often the CPU was stalled due to L2 cache
        accesses by loads]
       [(CYCLE_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L1D_PENDING - CYCLE_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L2_PENDING)
        / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_l2_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.2)]
  tma_l3_bound
       [This metric estimates how often the CPU was stalled due to loads
        accesses to L3 cache or contended with a sibling Core]
       [MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT / (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT + 7 *
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_MISS) * CYCLE_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L2_PENDING /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_l3_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.2)]

CacheMisses: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_info_memory_l2mpki_rfo
       [Offcore requests (L2 cache miss) per kilo instruction for demand RFOs]
       [1e3 * OFFCORE_REQUESTS.DEMAND_RFO / INST_RETIRED.ANY]

Compute: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_core_bound
       [This metric represents fraction of slots where Core non-memory issues
        were of a bottleneck]
       [tma_backend_bound - tma_memory_bound]
       [tma_core_bound > 0.1 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2]
  tma_port_0
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles CPU dispatched uops on
        execution port 0 ([SNB+] ALU; [HSW+] ALU and 2nd branch)]
       [UOPS_DISPATCHED_PORT.PORT_0 / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_port_0 > 0.6]

Cor: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_info_core_ilp
       [Instruction-Level-Parallelism (average number of uops executed when
        there is execution) per thread (logical-processor)]
       [(UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE / 2 / (cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=1@ / 2 if
        #SMT_on else cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=1@) if #SMT_on else
        UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE / (cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=1@ / 2 if #SMT_on
        else cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=1@))]

DSB: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_dsb
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles in which CPU was likely
        limited due to DSB (decoded uop cache) fetch pipeline]
       [(IDQ.ALL_DSB_CYCLES_ANY_UOPS - IDQ.ALL_DSB_CYCLES_4_UOPS) /
        tma_info_core_core_clks / 2]
       [tma_dsb > 0.15 & tma_fetch_bandwidth > 0.2]
  tma_info_frontend_dsb_coverage
       [Fraction of Uops delivered by the DSB (aka Decoded ICache; or Uop
        Cache)]
       [IDQ.DSB_UOPS / (IDQ.DSB_UOPS + LSD.UOPS + IDQ.MITE_UOPS + IDQ.MS_UOPS)]
       [tma_info_frontend_dsb_coverage < 0.7 & tma_info_thread_ipc / 4 > 0.35]

DSBmiss: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_dsb_switches
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        switches from DSB to MITE pipelines]
       [DSB2MITE_SWITCHES.PENALTY_CYCLES / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_dsb_switches > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)]
  tma_mite
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles in which CPU was likely
        limited due to the MITE pipeline (the legacy decode pipeline)]
       [(IDQ.ALL_MITE_CYCLES_ANY_UOPS - IDQ.ALL_MITE_CYCLES_4_UOPS) /
        tma_info_core_core_clks / 2]
       [tma_mite > 0.1 & tma_fetch_bandwidth > 0.2]

DataSharing: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_contested_accesses
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles while the memory subsystem
        was handling synchronizations due to contested accesses]
       [(60 * (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HITM * (1 +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.HIT_LFB / (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L2_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT + MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HITM +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_MISS +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_MISS))) + 43 *
        (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_MISS * (1 +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.HIT_LFB / (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L2_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT + MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HITM +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_MISS +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_MISS)))) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_contested_accesses > 0.05 & (tma_l3_bound > 0.05 &
        (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_false_sharing
       [This metric roughly estimates how often CPU was handling
        synchronizations due to False Sharing]
       [60 * OFFCORE_RESPONSE.DEMAND_RFO.L3_HIT.HITM_OTHER_CORE /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_false_sharing > 0.05 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound
        > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]

Default:
  CPUs_utilized
       [Average CPU utilization]
       [(software@cpu\-clock\,name\=cpu\-clock@ if #target_cpu else
        software@task\-clock\,name\=task\-clock@) / (duration_time * 1e9)]
  backend_cycles_idle
       [Backend stalls per cycle]
       [(stalled\-cycles\-backend / cpu\-cycles if
        has_event(stalled\-cycles\-backend) else 0)]
       [backend_cycles_idle > 0.2]
  branch_frequency
       [Branches per CPU second]
       [branches / (software@cpu\-clock\,name\=cpu\-clock@ if #target_cpu else
        software@task\-clock\,name\=task\-clock@)]
  branch_miss_rate
       [Branch miss rate]
       [branch\-misses / branches]
       [branch_miss_rate > 0.05]
  cs_per_second
       [Context switches per CPU second]
       [software@context\-switches\,name\=context\-switches@ * 1e9 /
        (software@cpu\-clock\,name\=cpu\-clock@ if #target_cpu else
        software@task\-clock\,name\=task\-clock@)]
  cycles_frequency
       [Cycles per CPU second]
       [cpu\-cycles / (software@cpu\-clock\,name\=cpu\-clock@ if #target_cpu
        else software@task\-clock\,name\=task\-clock@)]
  frontend_cycles_idle
       [Frontend stalls per cycle]
       [(stalled\-cycles\-frontend / cpu\-cycles if
        has_event(stalled\-cycles\-frontend) else 0)]
       [frontend_cycles_idle > 0.1]
  insn_per_cycle
       [Instructions Per Cycle]
       [instructions / cpu\-cycles]
       [insn_per_cycle < 1]
  migrations_per_second
       [Process migrations to a new CPU per CPU second]
       [software@cpu\-migrations\,name\=cpu\-migrations@ * 1e9 /
        (software@cpu\-clock\,name\=cpu\-clock@ if #target_cpu else
        software@task\-clock\,name\=task\-clock@)]
  page_faults_per_second
       [Page faults per CPU second]
       [software@page\-faults\,name\=page\-faults@ * 1e9 /
        (software@cpu\-clock\,name\=cpu\-clock@ if #target_cpu else
        software@task\-clock\,name\=task\-clock@)]
  stalled_cycles_per_instruction
       [Max front or backend stalls per instruction]
       [(max(stalled\-cycles\-frontend,stalled\-cycles\-backend) /
        instructions if has_event(stalled\-cycles\-frontend) &
        has_event(stalled\-cycles\-backend) else (stalled\-cycles\-frontend /
        instructions if has_event(stalled\-cycles\-frontend) else
        (stalled\-cycles\-backend / instructions if
        has_event(stalled\-cycles\-backend) else 0)))]

Default2:
  l1d_miss_rate
       [L1D miss rate]
       [L1\-dcache\-load\-misses / L1\-dcache\-loads]
       [l1d_miss_rate > 0.05]
  llc_miss_rate
       [LLC miss rate]
       [LLC\-load\-misses / LLC\-loads]
       [llc_miss_rate > 0.05]

Default3:
  dtlb_miss_rate
       [dTLB miss rate]
       [dTLB\-load\-misses / dTLB\-loads]
       [dtlb_miss_rate > 0.05]
  itlb_miss_rate
       [iTLB miss rate]
       [iTLB\-load\-misses / iTLB\-loads]
       [itlb_miss_rate > 0.05]
  l1i_miss_rate
       [L1I miss rate]
       [L1\-icache\-load\-misses / L1\-icache\-loads]
       [l1i_miss_rate > 0.05]

Default4:
  l1_prefetch_miss_rate
       [L1 prefetch miss rate]
       [L1\-dcache\-prefetch\-misses / L1\-dcache\-prefetches]
       [l1_prefetch_miss_rate > 0.05]

Fed: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_info_frontend_dsb_coverage
       [Fraction of Uops delivered by the DSB (aka Decoded ICache; or Uop
        Cache)]
       [IDQ.DSB_UOPS / (IDQ.DSB_UOPS + LSD.UOPS + IDQ.MITE_UOPS + IDQ.MS_UOPS)]
       [tma_info_frontend_dsb_coverage < 0.7 & tma_info_thread_ipc / 4 > 0.35]
  tma_info_frontend_ipunknown_branch
       [Instructions per speculative Unknown Branch Misprediction (BAClear)
        (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_instructions / BACLEARS.ANY]
  tma_info_inst_mix_bptkbranch
       [Branch instructions per taken branch]
       [BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES / BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_TAKEN]
  tma_info_inst_mix_ipbranch
       [Instructions per Branch (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [INST_RETIRED.ANY / BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_ipbranch < 8]
  tma_info_inst_mix_ipcall
       [Instructions per (near) call (lower number means higher occurrence
        rate)]
       [INST_RETIRED.ANY / BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_CALL]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_ipcall < 200]
  tma_info_inst_mix_iptb
       [Instructions per taken branch]
       [INST_RETIRED.ANY / BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_TAKEN]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_iptb < 9]
  tma_info_thread_uptb
       [Uops per taken branch]
       [UOPS_RETIRED.RETIRE_SLOTS / BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_TAKEN]
       [tma_info_thread_uptb < 6]

FetchBW: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_dsb
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles in which CPU was likely
        limited due to DSB (decoded uop cache) fetch pipeline]
       [(IDQ.ALL_DSB_CYCLES_ANY_UOPS - IDQ.ALL_DSB_CYCLES_4_UOPS) /
        tma_info_core_core_clks / 2]
       [tma_dsb > 0.15 & tma_fetch_bandwidth > 0.2]
  tma_fetch_bandwidth
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU was stalled due to
        Frontend bandwidth issues]
       [tma_frontend_bound - tma_fetch_latency]
       [tma_fetch_bandwidth > 0.2]
  tma_info_frontend_dsb_coverage
       [Fraction of Uops delivered by the DSB (aka Decoded ICache; or Uop
        Cache)]
       [IDQ.DSB_UOPS / (IDQ.DSB_UOPS + LSD.UOPS + IDQ.MITE_UOPS + IDQ.MS_UOPS)]
       [tma_info_frontend_dsb_coverage < 0.7 & tma_info_thread_ipc / 4 > 0.35]
  tma_info_frontend_tbpc
       [Taken Branches retired Per Cycle]
       [BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_TAKEN / tma_info_thread_clks]
  tma_info_inst_mix_iptb
       [Instructions per taken branch]
       [INST_RETIRED.ANY / BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_TAKEN]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_iptb < 9]
  tma_info_thread_uptb
       [Uops per taken branch]
       [UOPS_RETIRED.RETIRE_SLOTS / BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_TAKEN]
       [tma_info_thread_uptb < 6]
  tma_mite
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles in which CPU was likely
        limited due to the MITE pipeline (the legacy decode pipeline)]
       [(IDQ.ALL_MITE_CYCLES_ANY_UOPS - IDQ.ALL_MITE_CYCLES_4_UOPS) /
        tma_info_core_core_clks / 2]
       [tma_mite > 0.1 & tma_fetch_bandwidth > 0.2]

FetchLat: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_branch_resteers
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        Branch Resteers]
       [12 * (BR_MISP_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES + MACHINE_CLEARS.COUNT +
        BACLEARS.ANY) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_branch_resteers > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)]
  tma_dsb_switches
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        switches from DSB to MITE pipelines]
       [DSB2MITE_SWITCHES.PENALTY_CYCLES / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_dsb_switches > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)]
  tma_icache_misses
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        instruction cache misses]
       [ICACHE.IFDATA_STALL / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_icache_misses > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)]
  tma_itlb_misses
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        Instruction TLB (ITLB) misses]
       [(14 * ITLB_MISSES.STLB_HIT + ITLB_MISSES.WALK_DURATION) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_itlb_misses > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound
        > 0.15)]
  tma_lcp
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles CPU was stalled due to
        Length Changing Prefixes (LCPs)]
       [ILD_STALL.LCP / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_lcp > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)]
  tma_ms_switches
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles when the CPU was stalled
        due to switches of uop delivery to the Microcode Sequencer (MS)]
       [2 * IDQ.MS_SWITCHES / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_ms_switches > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound
        > 0.15)]

Frontend: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_fetch_bandwidth
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU was stalled due to
        Frontend bandwidth issues]
       [tma_frontend_bound - tma_fetch_latency]
       [tma_fetch_bandwidth > 0.2]
  tma_fetch_latency
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU was stalled due to
        Frontend latency issues]
       [4 * min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,
        IDQ_UOPS_NOT_DELIVERED.CYCLES_0_UOPS_DELIV.CORE) /
        tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15]
  tma_info_inst_mix_iptb
       [Instructions per taken branch]
       [INST_RETIRED.ANY / BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_TAKEN]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_iptb < 9]

HPC: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_info_system_cpu_utilization
       [Average CPU Utilization (percentage)]
       [tma_info_system_cpus_utilized / #num_cpus_online]
  tma_info_system_dram_bw_use
       [Average external Memory Bandwidth Use for reads and writes [GB / sec]]
       [64 * (UNC_ARB_TRK_REQUESTS.ALL + UNC_ARB_COH_TRK_REQUESTS.ALL) / 1e6 /
        tma_info_system_time / 1e3]

IcMiss: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_icache_misses
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        instruction cache misses]
       [ICACHE.IFDATA_STALL / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_icache_misses > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)]

InsType: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_info_inst_mix_ipbranch
       [Instructions per Branch (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [INST_RETIRED.ANY / BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_ipbranch < 8]
  tma_info_inst_mix_ipload
       [Instructions per Load (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [INST_RETIRED.ANY / MEM_UOPS_RETIRED.ALL_LOADS]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_ipload < 3]
  tma_info_inst_mix_ipstore
       [Instructions per Store (lower number means higher occurrence rate)]
       [INST_RETIRED.ANY / MEM_UOPS_RETIRED.ALL_STORES]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_ipstore < 8]

LockCont: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_contested_accesses
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles while the memory subsystem
        was handling synchronizations due to contested accesses]
       [(60 * (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HITM * (1 +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.HIT_LFB / (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L2_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT + MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HITM +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_MISS +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_MISS))) + 43 *
        (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_MISS * (1 +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.HIT_LFB / (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L2_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT + MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HITM +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_MISS +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_MISS)))) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_contested_accesses > 0.05 & (tma_l3_bound > 0.05 &
        (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_false_sharing
       [This metric roughly estimates how often CPU was handling
        synchronizations due to False Sharing]
       [60 * OFFCORE_RESPONSE.DEMAND_RFO.L3_HIT.HITM_OTHER_CORE /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_false_sharing > 0.05 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound
        > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_info_memory_latency_load_l2_miss_latency
       [Average Latency for L2 cache miss demand Loads]
       [OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.DEMAND_DATA_RD /
        OFFCORE_REQUESTS.DEMAND_DATA_RD]
  tma_lock_latency
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU spent handling cache
        misses due to lock operations]
       [MEM_UOPS_RETIRED.LOCK_LOADS / MEM_UOPS_RETIRED.ALL_STORES *
        min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,
        OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DEMAND_RFO) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_lock_latency > 0.2 & (tma_l1_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2
        & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_store_latency
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles the CPU spent handling L1D
        store misses]
       [(L2_RQSTS.RFO_HIT * 9 * (1 - MEM_UOPS_RETIRED.LOCK_LOADS /
        MEM_UOPS_RETIRED.ALL_STORES) + (1 - MEM_UOPS_RETIRED.LOCK_LOADS /
        MEM_UOPS_RETIRED.ALL_STORES) * min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,
        OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DEMAND_RFO)) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_store_latency > 0.1 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]

MachineClears: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_machine_clears
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU has wasted due to
        Machine Clears]
       [tma_bad_speculation - tma_branch_mispredicts]
       [tma_machine_clears > 0.1 & tma_bad_speculation > 0.15]

Mem:
  tma_info_memory_core_l1d_cache_fill_bw_2t
       [Average per-core data fill bandwidth to the L1 data cache [GB / sec]]
       [tma_info_memory_l1d_cache_fill_bw]
  tma_info_memory_core_l2_cache_fill_bw_2t
       [Average per-core data fill bandwidth to the L2 cache [GB / sec]]
       [tma_info_memory_l2_cache_fill_bw]
  tma_info_memory_core_l3_cache_fill_bw_2t
       [Average per-core data fill bandwidth to the L3 cache [GB / sec]]
       [tma_info_memory_l3_cache_fill_bw]
  tma_info_memory_l1d_cache_fill_bw
       [Average per-thread data fill bandwidth to the L1 data cache [GB / sec]]
       [64 * L1D.REPLACEMENT / 1e9 / tma_info_system_time]
  tma_info_memory_l1mpki
       [L1 cache true misses per kilo instruction for retired demand loads]
       [1e3 * MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L1_MISS / INST_RETIRED.ANY]
  tma_info_memory_l2_cache_fill_bw
       [Average per-thread data fill bandwidth to the L2 cache [GB / sec]]
       [64 * L2_LINES_IN.ALL / 1e9 / tma_info_system_time]
  tma_info_memory_l2mpki
       [L2 cache true misses per kilo instruction for retired demand loads]
       [1e3 * MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L2_MISS / INST_RETIRED.ANY]
  tma_info_memory_l3_cache_fill_bw
       [Average per-thread data fill bandwidth to the L3 cache [GB / sec]]
       [64 * LONGEST_LAT_CACHE.MISS / 1e9 / tma_info_system_time]
  tma_info_memory_l3mpki
       [L3 cache true misses per kilo instruction for retired demand loads]
       [1e3 * MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_MISS / INST_RETIRED.ANY]
  tma_info_memory_load_miss_real_latency
       [Actual Average Latency for L1 data-cache miss demand load operations
        (in core cycles)]
       [L1D_PEND_MISS.PENDING / (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L1_MISS +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.HIT_LFB)]
  tma_info_memory_mlp
       [Memory-Level-Parallelism (average number of L1 miss demand load when
        there is at least one such miss]
       [L1D_PEND_MISS.PENDING / L1D_PEND_MISS.PENDING_CYCLES]
  tma_info_memory_tlb_page_walks_utilization
       [Utilization of the core's Page Walker(s) serving STLB misses triggered
        by instruction/Load/Store accesses]
       [(ITLB_MISSES.WALK_DURATION + DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_DURATION +
        DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_DURATION) / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_info_memory_tlb_page_walks_utilization > 0.5]
  tma_info_thread_cpi
       [Cycles Per Instruction (per Logical Processor)]
       [1 / tma_info_thread_ipc]

MemOffcore: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_info_system_dram_bw_use
       [Average external Memory Bandwidth Use for reads and writes [GB / sec]]
       [64 * (UNC_ARB_TRK_REQUESTS.ALL + UNC_ARB_COH_TRK_REQUESTS.ALL) / 1e6 /
        tma_info_system_time / 1e3]

MemoryBW: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_fb_full
       [This metric does a *rough estimation* of how often L1D Fill Buffer
        unavailability limited additional L1D miss memory access requests to
        proceed]
       [tma_info_memory_load_miss_real_latency *
        cpu@L1D_PEND_MISS.REQUEST_FB_FULL\,cmask\=1@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_fb_full > 0.3]
  tma_info_memory_core_l1d_cache_fill_bw_2t
       [Average per-core data fill bandwidth to the L1 data cache [GB / sec]]
       [tma_info_memory_l1d_cache_fill_bw]
  tma_info_memory_core_l2_cache_fill_bw_2t
       [Average per-core data fill bandwidth to the L2 cache [GB / sec]]
       [tma_info_memory_l2_cache_fill_bw]
  tma_info_memory_core_l3_cache_fill_bw_2t
       [Average per-core data fill bandwidth to the L3 cache [GB / sec]]
       [tma_info_memory_l3_cache_fill_bw]
  tma_info_memory_l1d_cache_fill_bw
       [Average per-thread data fill bandwidth to the L1 data cache [GB / sec]]
       [64 * L1D.REPLACEMENT / 1e9 / tma_info_system_time]
  tma_info_memory_l2_cache_fill_bw
       [Average per-thread data fill bandwidth to the L2 cache [GB / sec]]
       [64 * L2_LINES_IN.ALL / 1e9 / tma_info_system_time]
  tma_info_memory_l3_cache_fill_bw
       [Average per-thread data fill bandwidth to the L3 cache [GB / sec]]
       [64 * LONGEST_LAT_CACHE.MISS / 1e9 / tma_info_system_time]
  tma_info_memory_mlp
       [Memory-Level-Parallelism (average number of L1 miss demand load when
        there is at least one such miss]
       [L1D_PEND_MISS.PENDING / L1D_PEND_MISS.PENDING_CYCLES]
  tma_info_system_dram_bw_use
       [Average external Memory Bandwidth Use for reads and writes [GB / sec]]
       [64 * (UNC_ARB_TRK_REQUESTS.ALL + UNC_ARB_COH_TRK_REQUESTS.ALL) / 1e6 /
        tma_info_system_time / 1e3]
  tma_mem_bandwidth
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles where the core's performance
        was likely hurt due to approaching bandwidth limits of external memory
        - DRAM ([SPR-HBM] and/or HBM)]
       [min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,
        cpu@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.ALL_DATA_RD\,cmask\=6@) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_mem_bandwidth > 0.2 & (tma_dram_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_sq_full
       [This metric measures fraction of cycles where the Super Queue (SQ) was
        full taking into account all request-types and both hardware SMT
        threads (Logical Processors)]
       [(OFFCORE_REQUESTS_BUFFER.SQ_FULL / 2 if #SMT_on else
        OFFCORE_REQUESTS_BUFFER.SQ_FULL) / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_sq_full > 0.3 & (tma_l3_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]

MemoryBound: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_dram_bound
       [This metric estimates how often the CPU was stalled on accesses to
        external memory (DRAM) by loads]
       [(1 - MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT / (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT + 7
        * MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_MISS)) * CYCLE_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L2_PENDING /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_dram_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.2)]
  tma_info_memory_load_miss_real_latency
       [Actual Average Latency for L1 data-cache miss demand load operations
        (in core cycles)]
       [L1D_PEND_MISS.PENDING / (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L1_MISS +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.HIT_LFB)]
  tma_info_memory_mlp
       [Memory-Level-Parallelism (average number of L1 miss demand load when
        there is at least one such miss]
       [L1D_PEND_MISS.PENDING / L1D_PEND_MISS.PENDING_CYCLES]
  tma_l1_bound
       [This metric estimates how often the CPU was stalled without loads
        missing the L1 Data (L1D) cache]
       [max((min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,CYCLE_ACTIVITY.STALLS_LDM_PENDING) -
        CYCLE_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L1D_PENDING) / tma_info_thread_clks,0)]
       [tma_l1_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.2)]
  tma_l2_bound
       [This metric estimates how often the CPU was stalled due to L2 cache
        accesses by loads]
       [(CYCLE_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L1D_PENDING - CYCLE_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L2_PENDING)
        / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_l2_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.2)]
  tma_l3_bound
       [This metric estimates how often the CPU was stalled due to loads
        accesses to L3 cache or contended with a sibling Core]
       [MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT / (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT + 7 *
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_MISS) * CYCLE_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L2_PENDING /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_l3_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.2)]
  tma_store_bound
       [This metric estimates how often CPU was stalled due to RFO store
        memory accesses; RFO store issue a read-for-ownership request before
        the write]
       [RESOURCE_STALLS.SB / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.2)]

MemoryLat: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_info_memory_load_miss_real_latency
       [Actual Average Latency for L1 data-cache miss demand load operations
        (in core cycles)]
       [L1D_PEND_MISS.PENDING / (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L1_MISS +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.HIT_LFB)]
  tma_l3_hit_latency
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles with demand load accesses
        that hit the L3 cache under unloaded scenarios (possibly L3 latency
        limited)]
       [29 * (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT * (1 +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.HIT_LFB / (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L2_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT + MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HITM +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_MISS +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_MISS))) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_l3_hit_latency > 0.1 & (tma_l3_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_mem_latency
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles where the performance was
        likely hurt due to latency from external memory - DRAM ([SPR-HBM]
        and/or HBM)]
       [min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,
        OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DATA_RD) /
        tma_info_thread_clks - tma_mem_bandwidth]
       [tma_mem_latency > 0.1 & (tma_dram_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_store_latency
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles the CPU spent handling L1D
        store misses]
       [(L2_RQSTS.RFO_HIT * 9 * (1 - MEM_UOPS_RETIRED.LOCK_LOADS /
        MEM_UOPS_RETIRED.ALL_STORES) + (1 - MEM_UOPS_RETIRED.LOCK_LOADS /
        MEM_UOPS_RETIRED.ALL_STORES) * min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,
        OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DEMAND_RFO)) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_store_latency > 0.1 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]

MemoryTLB: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_dtlb_load
       [This metric roughly estimates the fraction of cycles where the Data
        TLB (DTLB) was missed by load accesses]
       [(8 * DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.STLB_HIT + DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_DURATION) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_dtlb_load > 0.1 & (tma_l1_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_dtlb_store
       [This metric roughly estimates the fraction of cycles spent handling
        first-level data TLB store misses]
       [(8 * DTLB_STORE_MISSES.STLB_HIT + DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_DURATION) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_dtlb_store > 0.05 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_info_memory_tlb_page_walks_utilization
       [Utilization of the core's Page Walker(s) serving STLB misses triggered
        by instruction/Load/Store accesses]
       [(ITLB_MISSES.WALK_DURATION + DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_DURATION +
        DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_DURATION) / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_info_memory_tlb_page_walks_utilization > 0.5]
  tma_itlb_misses
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        Instruction TLB (ITLB) misses]
       [(14 * ITLB_MISSES.STLB_HIT + ITLB_MISSES.WALK_DURATION) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_itlb_misses > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound
        > 0.15)]

        OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DATA_RD]

MicroSeq: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_microcode_sequencer
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU was retiring uops
        fetched by the Microcode Sequencer (MS) unit]
       [UOPS_RETIRED.RETIRE_SLOTS / UOPS_ISSUED.ANY * IDQ.MS_UOPS /
        tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_microcode_sequencer > 0.05 & tma_heavy_operations > 0.1]
  tma_ms_switches
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles when the CPU was stalled
        due to switches of uop delivery to the Microcode Sequencer (MS)]
       [2 * IDQ.MS_SWITCHES / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_ms_switches > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound
        > 0.15)]

OS: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_info_system_ipfarbranch
       [Instructions per Far Branch ( Far Branches apply upon transition from
        application to operating system,handling interrupts,exceptions) [lower
        number means higher occurrence rate]]
       [INST_RETIRED.ANY / BR_INST_RETIRED.FAR_BRANCH:u]
       [tma_info_system_ipfarbranch < 1e6]

  tma_info_system_kernel_utilization
