       [CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD_P:k / CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD]
       [tma_info_system_kernel_utilization > 0.05]

Offcore: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_contested_accesses
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles while the memory subsystem
        was handling synchronizations due to contested accesses]
       [(60 * (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HITM * (1 +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.HIT_LFB / (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L2_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT + MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HITM +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_MISS +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_MISS))) + 43 *
        (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_MISS * (1 +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.HIT_LFB / (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L2_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT + MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HITM +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_MISS +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_MISS)))) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_contested_accesses > 0.05 & (tma_l3_bound > 0.05 &
        (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_data_sharing
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles while the memory subsystem
        was handling synchronizations due to data-sharing accesses]
       [43 * (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HIT * (1 +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.HIT_LFB / (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L2_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT + MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HITM +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_MISS +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_MISS))) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_data_sharing > 0.05 & (tma_l3_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_false_sharing
       [This metric roughly estimates how often CPU was handling
        synchronizations due to False Sharing]
       [60 * OFFCORE_RESPONSE.DEMAND_RFO.L3_HIT.HITM_OTHER_CORE /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_false_sharing > 0.05 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound
        > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_info_memory_l2mpki_rfo
       [Offcore requests (L2 cache miss) per kilo instruction for demand RFOs]
       [1e3 * OFFCORE_REQUESTS.DEMAND_RFO / INST_RETIRED.ANY]
  tma_info_memory_latency_data_l2_mlp
       [Average Parallel L2 cache miss data reads]
       [OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.ALL_DATA_RD /
        OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DATA_RD]
  tma_info_memory_latency_load_l2_miss_latency
       [Average Latency for L2 cache miss demand Loads]
       [OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.DEMAND_DATA_RD /
        OFFCORE_REQUESTS.DEMAND_DATA_RD]
  tma_info_memory_latency_load_l2_mlp
       [Average Parallel L2 cache miss demand Loads]
       [OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.DEMAND_DATA_RD /
        OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DEMAND_DATA_RD]
  tma_lock_latency
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU spent handling cache
        misses due to lock operations]
       [MEM_UOPS_RETIRED.LOCK_LOADS / MEM_UOPS_RETIRED.ALL_STORES *
        min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,
        OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DEMAND_RFO) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_lock_latency > 0.2 & (tma_l1_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2
        & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_mem_bandwidth
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles where the core's performance
        was likely hurt due to approaching bandwidth limits of external memory
        - DRAM ([SPR-HBM] and/or HBM)]
       [min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,
        cpu@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.ALL_DATA_RD\,cmask\=6@) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_mem_bandwidth > 0.2 & (tma_dram_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_mem_latency
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles where the performance was
        likely hurt due to latency from external memory - DRAM ([SPR-HBM]
        and/or HBM)]
       [min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,
        OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DATA_RD) /
        tma_info_thread_clks - tma_mem_bandwidth]
       [tma_mem_latency > 0.1 & (tma_dram_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_sq_full
       [This metric measures fraction of cycles where the Super Queue (SQ) was
        full taking into account all request-types and both hardware SMT
        threads (Logical Processors)]
       [(OFFCORE_REQUESTS_BUFFER.SQ_FULL / 2 if #SMT_on else
        OFFCORE_REQUESTS_BUFFER.SQ_FULL) / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_sq_full > 0.3 & (tma_l3_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_store_latency
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles the CPU spent handling L1D
        store misses]
       [(L2_RQSTS.RFO_HIT * 9 * (1 - MEM_UOPS_RETIRED.LOCK_LOADS /
        MEM_UOPS_RETIRED.ALL_STORES) + (1 - MEM_UOPS_RETIRED.LOCK_LOADS /
        MEM_UOPS_RETIRED.ALL_STORES) * min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,
        OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DEMAND_RFO)) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_store_latency > 0.1 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]

PGO: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_frontend_bound
       [This category represents fraction of slots where the processor's
        Frontend undersupplies its Backend]
       [IDQ_UOPS_NOT_DELIVERED.CORE / tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_frontend_bound > 0.15]
  tma_info_inst_mix_bptkbranch
       [Branch instructions per taken branch]
       [BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES / BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_TAKEN]
  tma_info_inst_mix_ipcall
       [Instructions per (near) call (lower number means higher occurrence
        rate)]
       [INST_RETIRED.ANY / BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_CALL]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_ipcall < 200]
  tma_info_inst_mix_iptb
       [Instructions per taken branch]
       [INST_RETIRED.ANY / BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_TAKEN]
       [tma_info_inst_mix_iptb < 9]

Pipeline: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_info_core_ilp
       [Instruction-Level-Parallelism (average number of uops executed when
        there is execution) per thread (logical-processor)]
       [(UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE / 2 / (cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=1@ / 2 if
        #SMT_on else cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=1@) if #SMT_on else
        UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE / (cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=1@ / 2 if #SMT_on
        else cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=1@))]
  tma_info_pipeline_retire
       [Average number of Uops retired in cycles where at least one uop has
        retired]
       [UOPS_RETIRED.RETIRE_SLOTS / cpu@UOPS_RETIRED.RETIRE_SLOTS\,cmask\=1@]
  tma_info_thread_clks
       [Per-Logical Processor actual clocks when the Logical Processor is
        active]
       [CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD]
  tma_info_thread_cpi
       [Cycles Per Instruction (per Logical Processor)]
       [1 / tma_info_thread_ipc]
  tma_info_thread_uoppi
       [Uops Per Instruction]
       [UOPS_RETIRED.RETIRE_SLOTS / INST_RETIRED.ANY]
       [tma_info_thread_uoppi > 1.05]

PortsUtil: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_info_core_ilp
       [Instruction-Level-Parallelism (average number of uops executed when
        there is execution) per thread (logical-processor)]
       [(UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE / 2 / (cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=1@ / 2 if
        #SMT_on else cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=1@) if #SMT_on else
        UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE / (cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=1@ / 2 if #SMT_on
        else cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=1@))]
  tma_ports_utilization
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles the CPU performance was
        potentially limited due to Core computation issues (non
        divider-related)]
       [((min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,CYCLE_ACTIVITY.CYCLES_NO_EXECUTE) +
        (cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=1@ - (cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,
        cmask\=3@ if tma_info_thread_ipc > 1.8 else cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,
        cmask\=2@)) / 2 - (RS_EVENTS.EMPTY_CYCLES if tma_fetch_latency > 0.1
        else 0) + RESOURCE_STALLS.SB if #SMT_on else
        min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,CYCLE_ACTIVITY.CYCLES_NO_EXECUTE) +
        cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=1@ - (cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=3@
        if tma_info_thread_ipc > 1.8 else cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=2@) -
        (RS_EVENTS.EMPTY_CYCLES if tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 else 0) +
        RESOURCE_STALLS.SB) - RESOURCE_STALLS.SB - min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,
        CYCLE_ACTIVITY.STALLS_LDM_PENDING)) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_ports_utilization > 0.15 & (tma_core_bound > 0.1 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.2)]
  tma_ports_utilized_0
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles CPU executed no uops on any
        execution port (Logical Processor cycles since ICL,Physical Core
        cycles otherwise)]
       [(cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,inv\,cmask\=1@ / 2 if #SMT_on else
        min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,CYCLE_ACTIVITY.CYCLES_NO_EXECUTE) -
        (RS_EVENTS.EMPTY_CYCLES if tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 else 0)) /
        tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_ports_utilized_0 > 0.2 & (tma_ports_utilization > 0.15 &
        (tma_core_bound > 0.1 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_ports_utilized_1
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles where the CPU executed total
        of 1 uop per cycle on all execution ports (Logical Processor cycles
        since ICL,Physical Core cycles otherwise)]
       [((cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=1@ - cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,
        cmask\=2@) / 2 if #SMT_on else cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=1@ -
        cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=2@) / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_ports_utilized_1 > 0.2 & (tma_ports_utilization > 0.15 &
        (tma_core_bound > 0.1 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_ports_utilized_2
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles CPU executed total of 2 uops
        per cycle on all execution ports (Logical Processor cycles since ICL,
        Physical Core cycles otherwise)]
       [((cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=2@ - cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,
        cmask\=3@) / 2 if #SMT_on else cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=2@ -
        cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=3@) / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_ports_utilized_2 > 0.15 & (tma_ports_utilization > 0.15 &
        (tma_core_bound > 0.1 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_ports_utilized_3m
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles CPU executed total of 3 or
        more uops per cycle on all execution ports (Logical Processor cycles
        since ICL,Physical Core cycles otherwise)]
       [(cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=3@ / 2 if #SMT_on else
        cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=3@) / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_ports_utilized_3m > 0.4 & (tma_ports_utilization > 0.15 &
        (tma_core_bound > 0.1 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]

Power: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  C2_Pkg_Residency
       [C2 residency percent per package]
       [cstate_pkg@c2\-residency@ / msr@tsc@]
  C3_Core_Residency
       [C3 residency percent per core]
       [cstate_core@c3\-residency@ / msr@tsc@]
  C3_Pkg_Residency
       [C3 residency percent per package]
       [cstate_pkg@c3\-residency@ / msr@tsc@]
  C6_Core_Residency
       [C6 residency percent per core]
       [cstate_core@c6\-residency@ / msr@tsc@]
  C6_Pkg_Residency
       [C6 residency percent per package]
       [cstate_pkg@c6\-residency@ / msr@tsc@]
  C7_Core_Residency
       [C7 residency percent per core]
       [cstate_core@c7\-residency@ / msr@tsc@]
  C7_Pkg_Residency
       [C7 residency percent per package]
       [cstate_pkg@c7\-residency@ / msr@tsc@]
  tma_info_system_core_frequency
       [Measured Average Core Frequency for unhalted processors [GHz]]
       [tma_info_system_turbo_utilization * msr@tsc@ / 1e9 /
        tma_info_system_time]
  tma_info_system_power
       [Total package Power in Watts]
       [power@energy\-pkg@ * 61 / (tma_info_system_time * 1e6)]
  tma_info_system_turbo_utilization
       [Average Frequency Utilization relative nominal frequency]
       [tma_info_thread_clks / CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.REF_TSC]

Ret: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_info_core_coreipc
       [Instructions Per Cycle across hyper-threads (per physical core)]
       [INST_RETIRED.ANY / tma_info_core_core_clks]
  tma_info_pipeline_retire
       [Average number of Uops retired in cycles where at least one uop has
        retired]
       [UOPS_RETIRED.RETIRE_SLOTS / cpu@UOPS_RETIRED.RETIRE_SLOTS\,cmask\=1@]
  tma_info_thread_ipc
       [Instructions Per Cycle (per Logical Processor)]
       [INST_RETIRED.ANY / tma_info_thread_clks]
  tma_info_thread_uoppi
       [Uops Per Instruction]
       [UOPS_RETIRED.RETIRE_SLOTS / INST_RETIRED.ANY]
       [tma_info_thread_uoppi > 1.05]

Retire: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_heavy_operations
       [This metric represents fraction of slots where the CPU was retiring
        heavy-weight operations -- instructions that require two or more uops
        or micro-coded sequences]
       [tma_microcode_sequencer]
       [tma_heavy_operations > 0.1]
  tma_info_thread_uoppi
       [Uops Per Instruction]
       [UOPS_RETIRED.RETIRE_SLOTS / INST_RETIRED.ANY]
       [tma_info_thread_uoppi > 1.05]
  tma_light_operations
       [This metric represents fraction of slots where the CPU was retiring
        light-weight operations -- instructions that require no more than one
        uop (micro-operation)]
       [tma_retiring - tma_heavy_operations]
       [tma_light_operations > 0.6]

SMT: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_info_core_core_clks
       [Core actual clocks when any Logical Processor is active on the
        Physical Core]
       [(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD / 2 * (1 + CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.ONE_THREAD_ACTIVE
        / CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.REF_XCLK) if #core_wide < 1 else
        (CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD_ANY / 2 if #SMT_on else
        tma_info_thread_clks))]
  tma_info_core_coreipc
       [Instructions Per Cycle across hyper-threads (per physical core)]
       [INST_RETIRED.ANY / tma_info_core_core_clks]
  tma_info_system_smt_2t_utilization
       [Fraction of cycles where both hardware Logical Processors were active]
       [(1 - CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.ONE_THREAD_ACTIVE /
        (CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.REF_XCLK_ANY / 2) if #SMT_on else 0)]

Snoop: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_contested_accesses
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles while the memory subsystem
        was handling synchronizations due to contested accesses]
       [(60 * (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HITM * (1 +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.HIT_LFB / (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L2_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT + MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HITM +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_MISS +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_MISS))) + 43 *
        (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_MISS * (1 +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.HIT_LFB / (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L2_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT + MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HITM +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_MISS +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_MISS)))) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_contested_accesses > 0.05 & (tma_l3_bound > 0.05 &
        (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_data_sharing
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles while the memory subsystem
        was handling synchronizations due to data-sharing accesses]
       [43 * (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HIT * (1 +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.HIT_LFB / (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L2_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT + MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HITM +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_MISS +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_MISS))) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_data_sharing > 0.05 & (tma_l3_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_false_sharing
       [This metric roughly estimates how often CPU was handling
        synchronizations due to False Sharing]
       [60 * OFFCORE_RESPONSE.DEMAND_RFO.L3_HIT.HITM_OTHER_CORE /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_false_sharing > 0.05 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound
        > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]

SoC: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  UNCORE_FREQ
       [Uncore frequency per die [GHZ]]
       [tma_info_system_socket_clks / #num_dies / duration_time / 1e9]
  tma_info_system_dram_bw_use
       [Average external Memory Bandwidth Use for reads and writes [GB / sec]]
       [64 * (UNC_ARB_TRK_REQUESTS.ALL + UNC_ARB_COH_TRK_REQUESTS.ALL) / 1e6 /
        tma_info_system_time / 1e3]
  tma_info_system_power
       [Total package Power in Watts]
       [power@energy\-pkg@ * 61 / (tma_info_system_time * 1e6)]
  tma_info_system_socket_clks
       [Socket actual clocks when any core is active on that socket]
       [UNC_CLOCK.SOCKET]

Summary: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_info_inst_mix_instructions
       [Total number of retired Instructions]
       [INST_RETIRED.ANY]
  tma_info_system_core_frequency
       [Measured Average Core Frequency for unhalted processors [GHz]]
       [tma_info_system_turbo_utilization * msr@tsc@ / 1e9 /
        tma_info_system_time]
  tma_info_system_cpu_utilization
       [Average CPU Utilization (percentage)]
       [tma_info_system_cpus_utilized / #num_cpus_online]
  tma_info_system_cpus_utilized
       [Average number of utilized CPUs]
       [CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.REF_TSC / msr@tsc@]
  tma_info_system_mux
       [PerfMon Event Multiplexing accuracy indicator]
       [CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD_P / CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD]
       [tma_info_system_mux > 1.1 | tma_info_system_mux < 0.9]
  tma_info_system_time
       [Run duration time in seconds]
       [duration_time]
       [tma_info_system_time < 1]
  tma_info_thread_ipc
       [Instructions Per Cycle (per Logical Processor)]
       [INST_RETIRED.ANY / tma_info_thread_clks]

TmaL1: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_backend_bound
       [This category represents fraction of slots where no uops are being
        delivered due to a lack of required resources for accepting new uops
        in the Backend]
       [1 - (tma_frontend_bound + tma_bad_speculation + tma_retiring)]
       [tma_backend_bound > 0.2]
  tma_bad_speculation
       [This category represents fraction of slots wasted due to incorrect
        speculations]
       [(UOPS_ISSUED.ANY - UOPS_RETIRED.RETIRE_SLOTS + 4 *
        (INT_MISC.RECOVERY_CYCLES_ANY / 2 if #SMT_on else
        INT_MISC.RECOVERY_CYCLES)) / tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_bad_speculation > 0.15]
  tma_frontend_bound
       [This category represents fraction of slots where the processor's
        Frontend undersupplies its Backend]
       [IDQ_UOPS_NOT_DELIVERED.CORE / tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_frontend_bound > 0.15]
  tma_info_core_coreipc
       [Instructions Per Cycle across hyper-threads (per physical core)]
       [INST_RETIRED.ANY / tma_info_core_core_clks]
  tma_info_inst_mix_instructions
       [Total number of retired Instructions]
       [INST_RETIRED.ANY]
  tma_info_thread_slots
       [Total issue-pipeline slots (per-Physical Core till ICL; per-Logical
        Processor ICL onward)]
       [4 * tma_info_core_core_clks]
  tma_retiring
       [This category represents fraction of slots utilized by useful work
        i.e. issued uops that eventually get retired]
       [UOPS_RETIRED.RETIRE_SLOTS / tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_retiring > 0.7 | tma_heavy_operations > 0.1]

TmaL2: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_branch_mispredicts
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU has wasted due to
        Branch Misprediction]
       [BR_MISP_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES / (BR_MISP_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES +
        MACHINE_CLEARS.COUNT) * tma_bad_speculation]
       [tma_branch_mispredicts > 0.1 & tma_bad_speculation > 0.15]
  tma_core_bound
       [This metric represents fraction of slots where Core non-memory issues
        were of a bottleneck]
       [tma_backend_bound - tma_memory_bound]
       [tma_core_bound > 0.1 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2]
  tma_fetch_bandwidth
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU was stalled due to
        Frontend bandwidth issues]
       [tma_frontend_bound - tma_fetch_latency]
       [tma_fetch_bandwidth > 0.2]
  tma_fetch_latency
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU was stalled due to
        Frontend latency issues]
       [4 * min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,
        IDQ_UOPS_NOT_DELIVERED.CYCLES_0_UOPS_DELIV.CORE) /
        tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15]
  tma_heavy_operations
       [This metric represents fraction of slots where the CPU was retiring
        heavy-weight operations -- instructions that require two or more uops
        or micro-coded sequences]
       [tma_microcode_sequencer]
       [tma_heavy_operations > 0.1]
  tma_light_operations
       [This metric represents fraction of slots where the CPU was retiring
        light-weight operations -- instructions that require no more than one
        uop (micro-operation)]
       [tma_retiring - tma_heavy_operations]
       [tma_light_operations > 0.6]
  tma_machine_clears
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU has wasted due to
        Machine Clears]
       [tma_bad_speculation - tma_branch_mispredicts]
       [tma_machine_clears > 0.1 & tma_bad_speculation > 0.15]
  tma_memory_bound
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the Memory subsystem within
        the Backend was a bottleneck]
       [(min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,CYCLE_ACTIVITY.STALLS_LDM_PENDING) +
        RESOURCE_STALLS.SB) / (min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,
        CYCLE_ACTIVITY.CYCLES_NO_EXECUTE) + (cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=1@
        - (cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=3@ if tma_info_thread_ipc > 1.8 else
        cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=2@)) / 2 - (RS_EVENTS.EMPTY_CYCLES if
        tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 else 0) + RESOURCE_STALLS.SB if #SMT_on else
        min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,CYCLE_ACTIVITY.CYCLES_NO_EXECUTE) +
        cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=1@ - (cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=3@
        if tma_info_thread_ipc > 1.8 else cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=2@) -
        (RS_EVENTS.EMPTY_CYCLES if tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 else 0) +
        RESOURCE_STALLS.SB) * tma_backend_bound]
       [tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2]

TmaL3mem: [Grouping from Top-down Microarchitecture Analysis Metrics spreadsheet]
  tma_dram_bound
       [This metric estimates how often the CPU was stalled on accesses to
        external memory (DRAM) by loads]
       [(1 - MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT / (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT + 7
        * MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_MISS)) * CYCLE_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L2_PENDING /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_dram_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.2)]
  tma_l1_bound
       [This metric estimates how often the CPU was stalled without loads
        missing the L1 Data (L1D) cache]
       [max((min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,CYCLE_ACTIVITY.STALLS_LDM_PENDING) -
        CYCLE_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L1D_PENDING) / tma_info_thread_clks,0)]
       [tma_l1_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.2)]
  tma_l2_bound
       [This metric estimates how often the CPU was stalled due to L2 cache
        accesses by loads]
       [(CYCLE_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L1D_PENDING - CYCLE_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L2_PENDING)
        / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_l2_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.2)]
  tma_l3_bound
       [This metric estimates how often the CPU was stalled due to loads
        accesses to L3 cache or contended with a sibling Core]
       [MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT / (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT + 7 *
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_MISS) * CYCLE_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L2_PENDING /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_l3_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.2)]
  tma_store_bound
       [This metric estimates how often CPU was stalled due to RFO store
        memory accesses; RFO store issue a read-for-ownership request before
        the write]
       [RESOURCE_STALLS.SB / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.2)]

TopdownL1: [Metrics for top-down breakdown at level 1]
  tma_backend_bound
       [This category represents fraction of slots where no uops are being
        delivered due to a lack of required resources for accepting new uops
        in the Backend]
       [1 - (tma_frontend_bound + tma_bad_speculation + tma_retiring)]
       [tma_backend_bound > 0.2]
  tma_bad_speculation
       [This category represents fraction of slots wasted due to incorrect
        speculations]
       [(UOPS_ISSUED.ANY - UOPS_RETIRED.RETIRE_SLOTS + 4 *
        (INT_MISC.RECOVERY_CYCLES_ANY / 2 if #SMT_on else
        INT_MISC.RECOVERY_CYCLES)) / tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_bad_speculation > 0.15]
  tma_frontend_bound
       [This category represents fraction of slots where the processor's
        Frontend undersupplies its Backend]
       [IDQ_UOPS_NOT_DELIVERED.CORE / tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_frontend_bound > 0.15]
  tma_retiring
       [This category represents fraction of slots utilized by useful work
        i.e. issued uops that eventually get retired]
       [UOPS_RETIRED.RETIRE_SLOTS / tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_retiring > 0.7 | tma_heavy_operations > 0.1]

TopdownL2: [Metrics for top-down breakdown at level 2]
  tma_branch_mispredicts
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU has wasted due to
        Branch Misprediction]
       [BR_MISP_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES / (BR_MISP_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES +
        MACHINE_CLEARS.COUNT) * tma_bad_speculation]
       [tma_branch_mispredicts > 0.1 & tma_bad_speculation > 0.15]
  tma_core_bound
       [This metric represents fraction of slots where Core non-memory issues
        were of a bottleneck]
       [tma_backend_bound - tma_memory_bound]
       [tma_core_bound > 0.1 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2]
  tma_fetch_bandwidth
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU was stalled due to
        Frontend bandwidth issues]
       [tma_frontend_bound - tma_fetch_latency]
       [tma_fetch_bandwidth > 0.2]
  tma_fetch_latency
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU was stalled due to
        Frontend latency issues]
       [4 * min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,
        IDQ_UOPS_NOT_DELIVERED.CYCLES_0_UOPS_DELIV.CORE) /
        tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15]
  tma_heavy_operations
       [This metric represents fraction of slots where the CPU was retiring
        heavy-weight operations -- instructions that require two or more uops
        or micro-coded sequences]
       [tma_microcode_sequencer]
       [tma_heavy_operations > 0.1]
  tma_light_operations
       [This metric represents fraction of slots where the CPU was retiring
        light-weight operations -- instructions that require no more than one
        uop (micro-operation)]
       [tma_retiring - tma_heavy_operations]
       [tma_light_operations > 0.6]
  tma_machine_clears
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU has wasted due to
        Machine Clears]
       [tma_bad_speculation - tma_branch_mispredicts]
       [tma_machine_clears > 0.1 & tma_bad_speculation > 0.15]
  tma_memory_bound
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the Memory subsystem within
        the Backend was a bottleneck]
       [(min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,CYCLE_ACTIVITY.STALLS_LDM_PENDING) +
        RESOURCE_STALLS.SB) / (min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,
        CYCLE_ACTIVITY.CYCLES_NO_EXECUTE) + (cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=1@
        - (cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=3@ if tma_info_thread_ipc > 1.8 else
        cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=2@)) / 2 - (RS_EVENTS.EMPTY_CYCLES if
        tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 else 0) + RESOURCE_STALLS.SB if #SMT_on else
        min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,CYCLE_ACTIVITY.CYCLES_NO_EXECUTE) +
        cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=1@ - (cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=3@
        if tma_info_thread_ipc > 1.8 else cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=2@) -
        (RS_EVENTS.EMPTY_CYCLES if tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 else 0) +
        RESOURCE_STALLS.SB) * tma_backend_bound]
       [tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2]

TopdownL3: [Metrics for top-down breakdown at level 3]
  tma_branch_resteers
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        Branch Resteers]
       [12 * (BR_MISP_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES + MACHINE_CLEARS.COUNT +
        BACLEARS.ANY) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_branch_resteers > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)]
  tma_divider
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles where the Divider unit was
        active]
       [10 * ARITH.DIVIDER_UOPS / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_divider > 0.2 & (tma_core_bound > 0.1 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2)]
  tma_dram_bound
       [This metric estimates how often the CPU was stalled on accesses to
        external memory (DRAM) by loads]
       [(1 - MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT / (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT + 7
        * MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_MISS)) * CYCLE_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L2_PENDING /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_dram_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.2)]
  tma_dsb
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles in which CPU was likely
        limited due to DSB (decoded uop cache) fetch pipeline]
       [(IDQ.ALL_DSB_CYCLES_ANY_UOPS - IDQ.ALL_DSB_CYCLES_4_UOPS) /
        tma_info_core_core_clks / 2]
       [tma_dsb > 0.15 & tma_fetch_bandwidth > 0.2]
  tma_dsb_switches
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        switches from DSB to MITE pipelines]
       [DSB2MITE_SWITCHES.PENALTY_CYCLES / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_dsb_switches > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)]
  tma_icache_misses
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        instruction cache misses]
       [ICACHE.IFDATA_STALL / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_icache_misses > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 &
        tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)]
  tma_itlb_misses
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU was stalled due to
        Instruction TLB (ITLB) misses]
       [(14 * ITLB_MISSES.STLB_HIT + ITLB_MISSES.WALK_DURATION) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_itlb_misses > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound
        > 0.15)]
  tma_l1_bound
       [This metric estimates how often the CPU was stalled without loads
        missing the L1 Data (L1D) cache]
       [max((min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,CYCLE_ACTIVITY.STALLS_LDM_PENDING) -
        CYCLE_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L1D_PENDING) / tma_info_thread_clks,0)]
       [tma_l1_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.2)]
  tma_l2_bound
       [This metric estimates how often the CPU was stalled due to L2 cache
        accesses by loads]
       [(CYCLE_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L1D_PENDING - CYCLE_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L2_PENDING)
        / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_l2_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.2)]
  tma_l3_bound
       [This metric estimates how often the CPU was stalled due to loads
        accesses to L3 cache or contended with a sibling Core]
       [MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT / (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT + 7 *
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_MISS) * CYCLE_ACTIVITY.STALLS_L2_PENDING /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_l3_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.2)]
  tma_lcp
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles CPU was stalled due to
        Length Changing Prefixes (LCPs)]
       [ILD_STALL.LCP / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_lcp > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound > 0.15)]
  tma_microcode_sequencer
       [This metric represents fraction of slots the CPU was retiring uops
        fetched by the Microcode Sequencer (MS) unit]
       [UOPS_RETIRED.RETIRE_SLOTS / UOPS_ISSUED.ANY * IDQ.MS_UOPS /
        tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_microcode_sequencer > 0.05 & tma_heavy_operations > 0.1]
  tma_mite
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles in which CPU was likely
        limited due to the MITE pipeline (the legacy decode pipeline)]
       [(IDQ.ALL_MITE_CYCLES_ANY_UOPS - IDQ.ALL_MITE_CYCLES_4_UOPS) /
        tma_info_core_core_clks / 2]
       [tma_mite > 0.1 & tma_fetch_bandwidth > 0.2]
  tma_ms_switches
       [This metric estimates the fraction of cycles when the CPU was stalled
        due to switches of uop delivery to the Microcode Sequencer (MS)]
       [2 * IDQ.MS_SWITCHES / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_ms_switches > 0.05 & (tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 & tma_frontend_bound
        > 0.15)]
  tma_ports_utilization
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles the CPU performance was
        potentially limited due to Core computation issues (non
        divider-related)]
       [((min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,CYCLE_ACTIVITY.CYCLES_NO_EXECUTE) +
        (cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=1@ - (cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,
        cmask\=3@ if tma_info_thread_ipc > 1.8 else cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,
        cmask\=2@)) / 2 - (RS_EVENTS.EMPTY_CYCLES if tma_fetch_latency > 0.1
        else 0) + RESOURCE_STALLS.SB if #SMT_on else
        min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,CYCLE_ACTIVITY.CYCLES_NO_EXECUTE) +
        cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=1@ - (cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=3@
        if tma_info_thread_ipc > 1.8 else cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=2@) -
        (RS_EVENTS.EMPTY_CYCLES if tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 else 0) +
        RESOURCE_STALLS.SB) - RESOURCE_STALLS.SB - min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,
        CYCLE_ACTIVITY.STALLS_LDM_PENDING)) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_ports_utilization > 0.15 & (tma_core_bound > 0.1 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.2)]
  tma_store_bound
       [This metric estimates how often CPU was stalled due to RFO store
        memory accesses; RFO store issue a read-for-ownership request before
        the write]
       [RESOURCE_STALLS.SB / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound >
        0.2)]

TopdownL4: [Metrics for top-down breakdown at level 4]
  tma_4k_aliasing
       [This metric estimates how often memory load accesses were aliased by
        preceding stores (in program order) with a 4K address offset]
       [LD_BLOCKS_PARTIAL.ADDRESS_ALIAS / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_4k_aliasing > 0.2 & (tma_l1_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2
        & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_assists
       [This metric estimates fraction of slots the CPU retired uops delivered
        by the Microcode_Sequencer as a result of Assists]
       [66 * OTHER_ASSISTS.ANY_WB_ASSIST / tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_assists > 0.1 & (tma_microcode_sequencer > 0.05 &
        tma_heavy_operations > 0.1)]
  tma_cisc
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles the CPU retired uops
        originated from CISC (complex instruction set computer) instruction]
       [max(0,tma_microcode_sequencer - tma_assists)]
       [tma_cisc > 0.1 & (tma_microcode_sequencer > 0.05 &
        tma_heavy_operations > 0.1)]
  tma_contested_accesses
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles while the memory subsystem
        was handling synchronizations due to contested accesses]
       [(60 * (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HITM * (1 +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.HIT_LFB / (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L2_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT + MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HITM +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_MISS +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_MISS))) + 43 *
        (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_MISS * (1 +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.HIT_LFB / (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L2_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT + MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HITM +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_MISS +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_MISS)))) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_contested_accesses > 0.05 & (tma_l3_bound > 0.05 &
        (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_data_sharing
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles while the memory subsystem
        was handling synchronizations due to data-sharing accesses]
       [43 * (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HIT * (1 +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.HIT_LFB / (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L2_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT + MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HITM +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_MISS +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_MISS))) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_data_sharing > 0.05 & (tma_l3_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_dtlb_load
       [This metric roughly estimates the fraction of cycles where the Data
        TLB (DTLB) was missed by load accesses]
       [(8 * DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.STLB_HIT + DTLB_LOAD_MISSES.WALK_DURATION) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_dtlb_load > 0.1 & (tma_l1_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_dtlb_store
       [This metric roughly estimates the fraction of cycles spent handling
        first-level data TLB store misses]
       [(8 * DTLB_STORE_MISSES.STLB_HIT + DTLB_STORE_MISSES.WALK_DURATION) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_dtlb_store > 0.05 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_false_sharing
       [This metric roughly estimates how often CPU was handling
        synchronizations due to False Sharing]
       [60 * OFFCORE_RESPONSE.DEMAND_RFO.L3_HIT.HITM_OTHER_CORE /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_false_sharing > 0.05 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound
        > 0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_fb_full
       [This metric does a *rough estimation* of how often L1D Fill Buffer
        unavailability limited additional L1D miss memory access requests to
        proceed]
       [tma_info_memory_load_miss_real_latency *
        cpu@L1D_PEND_MISS.REQUEST_FB_FULL\,cmask\=1@ / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_fb_full > 0.3]
  tma_l3_hit_latency
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles with demand load accesses
        that hit the L3 cache under unloaded scenarios (possibly L3 latency
        limited)]
       [29 * (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT * (1 +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.HIT_LFB / (MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L2_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_HIT + MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HIT +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_HITM +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_L3_HIT_RETIRED.XSNP_MISS +
        MEM_LOAD_UOPS_RETIRED.L3_MISS))) / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_l3_hit_latency > 0.1 & (tma_l3_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_lock_latency
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles the CPU spent handling cache
        misses due to lock operations]
       [MEM_UOPS_RETIRED.LOCK_LOADS / MEM_UOPS_RETIRED.ALL_STORES *
        min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,
        OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DEMAND_RFO) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_lock_latency > 0.2 & (tma_l1_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2
        & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_mem_bandwidth
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles where the core's performance
        was likely hurt due to approaching bandwidth limits of external memory
        - DRAM ([SPR-HBM] and/or HBM)]
       [min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,
        cpu@OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.ALL_DATA_RD\,cmask\=6@) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_mem_bandwidth > 0.2 & (tma_dram_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_mem_latency
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles where the performance was
        likely hurt due to latency from external memory - DRAM ([SPR-HBM]
        and/or HBM)]
       [min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,
        OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DATA_RD) /
        tma_info_thread_clks - tma_mem_bandwidth]
       [tma_mem_latency > 0.1 & (tma_dram_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_ports_utilized_0
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles CPU executed no uops on any
        execution port (Logical Processor cycles since ICL,Physical Core
        cycles otherwise)]
       [(cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,inv\,cmask\=1@ / 2 if #SMT_on else
        min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,CYCLE_ACTIVITY.CYCLES_NO_EXECUTE) -
        (RS_EVENTS.EMPTY_CYCLES if tma_fetch_latency > 0.1 else 0)) /
        tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_ports_utilized_0 > 0.2 & (tma_ports_utilization > 0.15 &
        (tma_core_bound > 0.1 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_ports_utilized_1
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles where the CPU executed total
        of 1 uop per cycle on all execution ports (Logical Processor cycles
        since ICL,Physical Core cycles otherwise)]
       [((cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=1@ - cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,
        cmask\=2@) / 2 if #SMT_on else cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=1@ -
        cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=2@) / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_ports_utilized_1 > 0.2 & (tma_ports_utilization > 0.15 &
        (tma_core_bound > 0.1 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_ports_utilized_2
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles CPU executed total of 2 uops
        per cycle on all execution ports (Logical Processor cycles since ICL,
        Physical Core cycles otherwise)]
       [((cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=2@ - cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,
        cmask\=3@) / 2 if #SMT_on else cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=2@ -
        cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=3@) / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_ports_utilized_2 > 0.15 & (tma_ports_utilization > 0.15 &
        (tma_core_bound > 0.1 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_ports_utilized_3m
       [This metric represents fraction of cycles CPU executed total of 3 or
        more uops per cycle on all execution ports (Logical Processor cycles
        since ICL,Physical Core cycles otherwise)]
       [(cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=3@ / 2 if #SMT_on else
        cpu@UOPS_EXECUTED.CORE\,cmask\=3@) / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_ports_utilized_3m > 0.4 & (tma_ports_utilization > 0.15 &
        (tma_core_bound > 0.1 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_split_loads
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles handling memory load split
        accesses - load that cross 64-byte cache line boundary]
       [tma_info_memory_load_miss_real_latency * LD_BLOCKS.NO_SR /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_split_loads > 0.3]
  tma_split_stores
       [This metric represents rate of split store accesses]
       [2 * MEM_UOPS_RETIRED.SPLIT_STORES / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_split_stores > 0.2 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_sq_full
       [This metric measures fraction of cycles where the Super Queue (SQ) was
        full taking into account all request-types and both hardware SMT
        threads (Logical Processors)]
       [(OFFCORE_REQUESTS_BUFFER.SQ_FULL / 2 if #SMT_on else
        OFFCORE_REQUESTS_BUFFER.SQ_FULL) / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_sq_full > 0.3 & (tma_l3_bound > 0.05 & (tma_memory_bound > 0.2 &
        tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_store_fwd_blk
       [This metric roughly estimates fraction of cycles when the memory
        subsystem had loads blocked since they could not forward data from
        earlier (in program order) overlapping stores]
       [13 * LD_BLOCKS.STORE_FORWARD / tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_store_fwd_blk > 0.1 & (tma_l1_bound > 0.1 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]
  tma_store_latency
       [This metric estimates fraction of cycles the CPU spent handling L1D
        store misses]
       [(L2_RQSTS.RFO_HIT * 9 * (1 - MEM_UOPS_RETIRED.LOCK_LOADS /
        MEM_UOPS_RETIRED.ALL_STORES) + (1 - MEM_UOPS_RETIRED.LOCK_LOADS /
        MEM_UOPS_RETIRED.ALL_STORES) * min(CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD,
        OFFCORE_REQUESTS_OUTSTANDING.CYCLES_WITH_DEMAND_RFO)) /
        tma_info_thread_clks]
       [tma_store_latency > 0.1 & (tma_store_bound > 0.2 & (tma_memory_bound >
        0.2 & tma_backend_bound > 0.2))]

TopdownL5: [Metrics for top-down breakdown at level 5]
  tma_alu_op_utilization
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles CPU dispatched uops on
        execution ports for ALU operations]
       [(UOPS_DISPATCHED_PORT.PORT_0 + UOPS_DISPATCHED_PORT.PORT_1 +
        UOPS_DISPATCHED_PORT.PORT_5 + UOPS_DISPATCHED_PORT.PORT_6) /
        tma_info_thread_slots]
       [tma_alu_op_utilization > 0.4]
  tma_load_op_utilization
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles CPU dispatched uops on
        execution port for Load operations]
       [(UOPS_DISPATCHED_PORT.PORT_2 + UOPS_DISPATCHED_PORT.PORT_3 +
        UOPS_DISPATCHED_PORT.PORT_7 - UOPS_DISPATCHED_PORT.PORT_4) / (2 *
        tma_info_core_core_clks)]
       [tma_load_op_utilization > 0.6]
  tma_store_op_utilization
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles CPU dispatched uops on
        execution port for Store operations]
       [UOPS_DISPATCHED_PORT.PORT_4 / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_store_op_utilization > 0.6]

TopdownL6: [Metrics for top-down breakdown at level 6]
  tma_port_0
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles CPU dispatched uops on
        execution port 0 ([SNB+] ALU; [HSW+] ALU and 2nd branch)]
       [UOPS_DISPATCHED_PORT.PORT_0 / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_port_0 > 0.6]
  tma_port_1
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles CPU dispatched uops on
        execution port 1 (ALU)]
       [UOPS_DISPATCHED_PORT.PORT_1 / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_port_1 > 0.6]
  tma_port_2
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles CPU dispatched uops on
        execution port 2 ([SNB+]Loads and Store-address; [ICL+] Loads)]
       [UOPS_DISPATCHED_PORT.PORT_2 / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_port_2 > 0.6]
  tma_port_3
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles CPU dispatched uops on
        execution port 3 ([SNB+]Loads and Store-address; [ICL+] Loads)]
       [UOPS_DISPATCHED_PORT.PORT_3 / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_port_3 > 0.6]
  tma_port_4
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles CPU dispatched uops on
        execution port 4 (Store-data)]
       [tma_store_op_utilization]
       [tma_port_4 > 0.6]
  tma_port_5
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles CPU dispatched uops on
        execution port 5 ([SNB+] Branches and ALU; [HSW+] ALU)]
       [UOPS_DISPATCHED_PORT.PORT_5 / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_port_5 > 0.6]
  tma_port_6
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles CPU dispatched uops on
        execution port 6 ([HSW+] Primary Branch and simple ALU)]
       [UOPS_DISPATCHED_PORT.PORT_6 / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_port_6 > 0.6]
  tma_port_7
       [This metric represents Core fraction of cycles CPU dispatched uops on
        execution port 7 ([HSW+]simple Store-address)]
       [UOPS_DISPATCHED_PORT.PORT_7 / tma_info_core_core_clks]
       [tma_port_7 > 0.6]

lpm_br: [breakdown of retired branch instructions]
  lpm_br_cond_insn_between_branches
       [The number of instructions divided by the number of conditional
        branches]
       [d_ratio(instructions,BR_INST_RETIRED.CONDITIONAL)]
  lpm_br_cond_mispred
       [Retired conditional branch instructions mispredicted as a percentage
        of all conditional branches]
       [d_ratio(BR_MISP_RETIRED.CONDITIONAL,BR_INST_RETIRED.CONDITIONAL)]
  lpm_br_cond_retired
       [Retired conditional branch instructions]
       [d_ratio(BR_INST_RETIRED.CONDITIONAL,duration_time)]
  lpm_br_far_insn_between_branches
       [The number of instructions divided by the number of far branches]
       [d_ratio(instructions,BR_INST_RETIRED.FAR_BRANCH)]
  lpm_br_far_retired
       [Retired far control transfers per second]
       [d_ratio(BR_INST_RETIRED.FAR_BRANCH,duration_time)]
  lpm_br_taken_insn_between_branches
       [The number of instructions divided by the number of taken branches]
       [d_ratio(instructions,BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES)]
  lpm_br_taken_mispred
       [The number of retired taken branch instructions that were mispredicted
        as a percentage of all taken branches]
       [d_ratio(BR_MISP_RETIRED.NEAR_TAKEN,BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES)]
  lpm_br_taken_retired
       [The number of taken branches that were retired per second]
       [d_ratio(BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES,duration_time)]
  lpm_br_total_insn_between_branches
       [The number of instructions divided by the number of branches]
       [d_ratio(instructions,BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES)]
  lpm_br_total_insn_fe_resteers
       [The number of resync branches per second]
       [d_ratio(BACLEARS.ANY,duration_time)]
  lpm_br_total_mispred
       [The number of branch instructions retired,of any type,that were not
        correctly predicted as a percentage of all branch instrucions]
       [d_ratio(BR_MISP_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES,BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES)]
  lpm_br_total_retired
       [The number of branch instructions retired per second]
       [d_ratio(BR_INST_RETIRED.ALL_BRANCHES,duration_time)]

lpm_cpu_power: [Running Average Power Limit (RAPL) power consumption estimates]
  lpm_cpu_power_cores
       [d_ratio((power@energy\-cores@ if has_event(power@energy\-cores@) else
        NaN) * 232.83064365386963e-12,duration_time)]
  lpm_cpu_power_gpu
       [d_ratio((power@energy\-gpu@ if has_event(power@energy\-gpu@) else NaN)
        * 232.83064365386963e-12,duration_time)]
  lpm_cpu_power_pkg
       [d_ratio((power@energy\-pkg@ if has_event(power@energy\-pkg@) else NaN)
        * 232.83064365386963e-12,duration_time)]
  lpm_cpu_power_psys
       [d_ratio((power@energy\-psys@ if has_event(power@energy\-psys@) else
        NaN) * 232.83064365386963e-12,duration_time)]
  lpm_cpu_power_ram
       [d_ratio((power@energy\-ram@ if has_event(power@energy\-ram@) else NaN)
        * 232.83064365386963e-12,duration_time)]

lpm_cs: [Number of context switches per second, instructions retired & core cycles between context switches]
  lpm_cs_br_taken
       [Branches taken per context switch]
       [d_ratio(BR_INST_RETIRED.NEAR_TAKEN,context\-switches)]
  lpm_cs_cycles
       [Cycles per context switch]
       [d_ratio(cycles,context\-switches)]
  lpm_cs_instr
       [Instructions per context switch]
       [d_ratio(instructions,context\-switches)]
  lpm_cs_l2_misses
       [L2 misses per context switch]
       [d_ratio(L2_RQSTS.DEMAND_DATA_RD_MISS + L2_RQSTS.RFO_MISS +
        L2_RQSTS.CODE_RD_MISS + L2_RQSTS.L2_PF_MISS,context\-switches)]
  lpm_cs_loads
       [Loads per context switch]
       [d_ratio(MEM_UOPS_RETIRED.ALL_LOADS,context\-switches)]
  lpm_cs_rate
       [Context switches per second]
       [d_ratio(context\-switches,duration_time)]
  lpm_cs_stores
       [Stores per context switch]
       [d_ratio(MEM_UOPS_RETIRED.ALL_STORES,context\-switches)]

lpm_cycles: [cycles breakdown per privilege level (users, kernel, guest)]
  lpm_cycles_guest
       [Hypervisor guest cycles as a percentage of all cycles]
       [d_ratio(cpu\-cycles:G,cpu\-cycles:kHh + cpu\-cycles:G +
        cpu\-cycles:uH)]
  lpm_cycles_kernel
       [Kernel cycles as a percentage of all cycles]
       [d_ratio(cpu\-cycles:kHh,cpu\-cycles:kHh + cpu\-cycles:G +
        cpu\-cycles:uH)]
  lpm_cycles_total
       [Total number of cycles]
       [cpu\-cycles:kHh + cpu\-cycles:G + cpu\-cycles:uH]
  lpm_cycles_user
       [User cycles as a percentage of all cycles]
       [d_ratio(cpu\-cycles:uH,cpu\-cycles:kHh + cpu\-cycles:G +
        cpu\-cycles:uH)]

lpm_idle:
  lpm_idle
       [Percentage of total wallclock cycles where CPUs are in low power state
        (C1 or deeper sleep state)]
       [d_ratio(max(msr@tsc@ - msr@mperf@,0),msr@tsc@)]

lpm_ilp:
  lpm_ilp_idle
       [Lower power cycles as a percentage of all cycles]
       [d_ratio(msr@tsc@ - msr@mperf@,msr@tsc@)]
  lpm_ilp_inst_ret_0
       [Instructions retired in 0 cycles as a percentage of all cycles]
       [1 - d_ratio(max(INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=1@ -
        INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=2@,0),CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD_P_ANY) -
        d_ratio(max(INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=2@ -
        INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=3@,0),CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD_P_ANY) -
        d_ratio(max(INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=3@ -
        INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=4@,0),CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD_P_ANY) -
        d_ratio(max(INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=4@ -
        INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=5@,0),CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD_P_ANY) -
        d_ratio(INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=5@,CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD_P_ANY)]
  lpm_ilp_inst_ret_1
       [Instructions retired in 1 cycles as a percentage of all cycles]
       [d_ratio(max(INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=1@ -
        INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=2@,0),CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD_P_ANY)]
  lpm_ilp_inst_ret_2
       [Instructions retired in 2 cycles as a percentage of all cycles]
       [d_ratio(max(INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=2@ -
        INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=3@,0),CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD_P_ANY)]
  lpm_ilp_inst_ret_3
       [Instructions retired in 3 cycles as a percentage of all cycles]
       [d_ratio(max(INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=3@ -
        INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=4@,0),CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD_P_ANY)]
  lpm_ilp_inst_ret_4
       [Instructions retired in 4 cycles as a percentage of all cycles]
       [d_ratio(max(INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=4@ -
        INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=5@,0),CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD_P_ANY)]
  lpm_ilp_inst_ret_5
       [Instructions retired in 5 or more cycles as a percentage of all cycles]
       [d_ratio(INST_RETIRED.ANY_P@cmask\=5@,CPU_CLK_UNHALTED.THREAD_P_ANY)]

lpm_l2: [L2 data cache analysis]
  lpm_l2_code_hits
       [L2 cache code hits]
       [d_ratio(L2_RQSTS.CODE_RD_HIT,L2_RQSTS.CODE_RD_HIT +
        L2_RQSTS.CODE_RD_MISS)]
  lpm_l2_code_misses
       [L2 cache code misses per second]
       [d_ratio(L2_RQSTS.CODE_RD_MISS,duration_time)]
  lpm_l2_code_requests
       [L2 cache code requests per second]
       [d_ratio(L2_RQSTS.CODE_RD_HIT + L2_RQSTS.CODE_RD_MISS,duration_time)]
  lpm_l2_rd_hits
       [L2 cache data read hits]
       [d_ratio(L2_RQSTS.DEMAND_DATA_RD_MISS,L2_RQSTS.DEMAND_DATA_RD_MISS +
        L2_RQSTS.DEMAND_DATA_RD_HIT)]
  lpm_l2_rd_misses
       [L2 cache data read misses per second]
       [d_ratio(L2_RQSTS.DEMAND_DATA_RD_MISS,duration_time)]
  lpm_l2_rd_requests
       [L2 cache data read requests per second]
       [d_ratio(L2_RQSTS.DEMAND_DATA_RD_MISS + L2_RQSTS.DEMAND_DATA_RD_HIT,
        duration_time)]
  lpm_l2_rfo_hits
       [L2 cache request for ownership (RFO) hits]
       [d_ratio(L2_RQSTS.RFO_HIT,L2_RQSTS.RFO_HIT + L2_RQSTS.RFO_MISS)]
  lpm_l2_rfo_misses
       [L2 cache request for ownership (RFO) misses per second]
       [d_ratio(L2_RQSTS.RFO_MISS,duration_time)]
  lpm_l2_rfo_requests
       [L2 cache request for ownership (RFO) requests per second]
       [d_ratio(L2_RQSTS.RFO_HIT + L2_RQSTS.RFO_MISS,duration_time)]
  lpm_l2_totals_in
       [L2 cache total in per second]
       [d_ratio(L2_LINES_IN.ALL,duration_time)]
  lpm_l2_totals_out
       [L2 cache total out per second]
       [d_ratio((L2_LINES_OUT.DEMAND_CLEAN / 2 if #smt_on else
        L2_LINES_OUT.DEMAND_CLEAN) + L2_LINES_OUT.DEMAND_DIRTY,duration_time)]

lpm_mlp:
